# TRUCK SPORTS — COMPLETE PRE-0.9.0 BACKLOG INVENTORY

Snapshot: November16,2026. This is the explicitly requested historical inventory, **not a new execution queue or competing ledger**. `TRUCK_SPORTS_ACCEPTANCE_LEDGER.md` remains authoritative. Only Release Foundation is active. Older task ordering, scenery/perfection work and recursive validation chains are superseded.

## Totals and interpretation

- 635 unique formal requirement records were read from the existing ledger; 275 recorded VERIFIED COMPLETE entries are retained and not reopened.
- 360 formal records remain open as recorded: 54 PARTIAL; 305 IMPLEMENTED BUT UNVERIFIED; 1 BLOCKED. None of these statuses is converted into a newly observed gameplay failure.
- Thirteen previously listed foundation subrequirements are separately summarized below; they are children of RF09-001, not additional pre-0.9 defects.
- Fifty future sports are retained as deferred content requirements, now0.10/0.11. Their detailed original source prompts were not found in the request directory; this is an explicit source-record limitation.
- The49unchecked Goddess checklist entries are cross-referenced in an appendix as historical records. Old unchecked boxes are not treated as new defects or counted twice.

Some earlier broad ledger entries remain unverified even where later narrower software checks passed. Their existing proof is retained; this snapshot does not schedule reruns. Human listening, hardware and balance acceptance remain distinct from automated results. Repeated evidence/debugger work is not a product feature.

## Current closeout corrections

- Hoard: V638 focused traffic regression passed; V639 ordinary match failed with zero banked points. The bots repeatedly reject obstructed escape courses. This is a real unresolved AI issue, deferred under the latest directive unless it blocks foundation flow.
- Egg: V632 completed both journeys/deliveries/results/rematch. Screenshot filenames were shortened in the preserved source. Missing original captures are not claimed recovered.
- Riders609 and Escape624 retain their existing passing match/rematch evidence. No repeat tests are requested by this inventory.
- Current0.8 source/version repairs are committed; isolated candidate world/build remains local, and no Windows launch or promoted0.8 release is certified here.
- Git preservation push succeeded at5c7b7aa85565fd0e66f6a7bfc413964d56029962 on the development branch. Stable branches remain unchanged.

## Open requirements grouped by version/system

Each entry provides the permanent ID, original version/system, affected sport/feature, outstanding scope, recorded implementation status, implementation file/scene evidence, existing test evidence, foundation-blocking assessment and recommended milestone. Long inherited prose is summarized; the exact original entry remains linked by line.

## Explicit non-goals and unknowns

- No player-facing played-sports checklist, board progression or separate party-pack products is authorized. Backend match analytics are required.
- Old scenery/palette/seating/cavern/compiler-lineage jobs remain preserved but their execution order is superseded. They are not0.9 acceptance gates.
- Intermittent Unity native accessibility shutdown remains a recorded engine issue (Q22-SKY-NATIVE-QUIT-01); it is not claimed fixed. Address only a reproduced release-flow failure with a bounded correction.
- No fresh gameplay, hardware, listening or Windows test was performed to generate this inventory. No verified entry was reopened.
- Current0.8 isolated candidate build and world-data export are not certified as delivered. The safe existing0.7 installation is retained.
- This inventory does not start the50future sports or any earlier deferred gameplay repair. Proceed only with Release Foundation.
